To overcome these limitations, we present DL-SLAM, a monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM system built upon a novel dual-level probabilistic framework. Our method computes dynamic probability maps by combining semantic and geometric information. These pixel-level probabilities are lifted to 3D and aggregated to derive an object-level dynamic probability for each instance. Object-level probability enables the categorical pruning of dynamic Gaussians, resulting in an artifact-free static map. The static map, in turn, provides a geometrically consistent guidance to refine the pixel-wise probabilities, enhancing their reliability. Experimental results demonstrate that DL-SLAM outperforms existing approaches, improving tracking accuracy by up to 13\% while generating high-fidelity semantic maps.

</details>


### [288] [SPLC: Social Preference Learning for Crowd Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01925)
*Zixuan Chen,Hao Fu,Haiwen Hu,Shiquan Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出SPLC算法，通过社会偏好反馈机制自动生成偏好数据，消除手动设计奖励函数的需求，在离线强化学习框架下实现更优的人群机器人导航性能，并通过真实环境实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人运动模式的复杂性导致社交合规机器人行为所需的奖励函数设计困难，现有方法难以平衡社会规范量化与算法性能优化。

Method: 创新性引入社会偏好反馈机制，基于原则性偏好评估标准自动生成偏好数据，显式建模行人动力学特征，构建系统化社会规范量化流程。

Result: 结合离线强化学习方法的SPLC在标准性能指标上持续超越现有最先进基线，TurtleBot4真实环境实验进一步验证其实用场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: SPLC成功解决奖励函数设计难题，通过自动偏好生成降低人为偏差，实现社会规范的系统化量化，为人类-机器人共存应用提供了可扩展的解决方案，相关代码和演示已开源。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) holds significant potential for crowd robot navigation in human-robot coexistence applications. However, the inherent complexity of pedestrian motion renders the design of effective reward functions for promoting socially compliant robot behaviors a persistent challenge. This paper proposes a Social Preference Learning for Crowd Robot Navigation (SPLC) algorithm to eliminate the need for detailed reward design. Its core innovation lies in the introduction of a social preference feedback mechanism to automatically generate preference data through principled preference evaluation criteria. By explicitly accounting for the intricacies of pedestrian dynamics, the pipeline mitigates the reward bias and facilitates the systematic quantification of broad social norms, thereby fostering socially compliant behaviors. Extensive experiments integrating SPLC with offline RL methods demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines across standard performance metrics. Furthermore, real-world experiments on the TurtleBot4 further validate the effectiveness of SPLC in practical human-robot coexistence settings. Our code and video demos are available at https://github.com/sklus949/SPLC.

</details>


### [289] [PhysMani: Physics-principled 3D World Model for Dynamic Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01938)
*Peng Yun,Shouwang Huang,Hao Li,Jinxi Li,Jianan Wang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出PhysMani框架，通过结合物理原理的3D高斯世界模型与未来感知行动策略模型，显著提升了在非结构化3D环境中对快速移动目标的操纵能力。在自建的动态操纵基准PhysMani-Bench上验证了其仿真与真实机器人任务中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言行动模型和世界模型在处理非结构化3D环境中的快速动态目标操纵时，存在3D几何建模不准确和物理约束不足的局限性，导致预测和决策可靠性低。

Method: PhysMani框架包含两个核心组件：1) 物理原理驱动的3D高斯世界模型，通过在线优化学习无散度高斯速度场以预测未来动态；2) 未来感知行动策略模型，利用可学习标记的交叉注意机制融合预测的动态场景信息。同时构建了含16项任务的PhysMani-Bench动态操纵基准进行验证。

Result: 在PhysMani-Bench的16个动态操纵任务中，PhysMani在仿真和真实机器人实验中均取得显著更高的成功率，超越现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的PhysMani框架有效解决了动态环境中的精确3D几何建模和物理约束预测问题，为具身AI的动态操纵提供了更高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Manipulating fast and dynamically moving targets in unstructured 3D environments remains challenging for embodied AI. Existing visual-language-action models and world models struggle with accurate 3D geometry and physically meaningful forecasting. We propose PhysMani, a framework that couples a physics-principled 3D Gaussian world model with a future-aware action policy model. The world model learns a divergence-free Gaussian velocity field via online optimization for fast and physically grounded future dynamics prediction. The policy model integrates the predicted 3D scene future dynamics through a learnable token based cross-attention module. We introduce PhysMani-Bench, a dynamic manipulation benchmark with 16 tasks, and demonstrate a superior success rate over strong baselines in both simulation and real-world robot experiments.

</details>


### [290] [A Stereo Visual SLAM System Using Object-Level Motion Estimation and Geometric Filtering Based on Cross Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02005)
*Sujan Kumar Dhali,Bhaskar Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OCD SLAM is a dynamic stereo SLAM system that improves upon ORB-SLAM2 by detecting and filtering dynamic features/objects using cross-disparity analysis and object detection/tracking, achieving better trajectory accuracy in dynamic environments.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard visual SLAM systems fail in dynamic environments due to their static-world assumption during pose estimation and mapping. This leads to inaccuracies when moving objects are present.

Method: Proposes a geometric approach using "cross disparity" (combining temporal and stereo inconsistency) to identify dynamic features, combined with SMOKE 3D object detection and Kalman filter-based tracking for object-level motion classification.

Result: Evaluated on KITTI datasets: shows significant trajectory accuracy improvements over ORB-SLAM2 and other dynamic SLAM methods. Ablation studies confirm cross-disparity's effectiveness in detecting features missed by object detection alone.

Conclusion: OCD SLAM robustly separates static/dynamic elements through complementary feature-level and object-level motion analysis, enhancing SLAM performance in dynamic scenes.

Abstract: This paper presents OCD SLAM, a dynamic stereo visual SLAM framework that extends ORB-SLAM2 by jointly addressing dynamic objects and dynamic features in the scene. Usual visual SLAM systems operating in dynamic environments often fail in the presence of moving objects, due to the static-world assumption used in pose estimation and mapping. To address this predicament, we introduce a novel geometric approach based on the discrepancy between disparity and a newly proposed notion called ``cross disparity'', which exploits both temporal and stereo inconsistency to identify dynamic feature points. Complementary to this feature-level motion analysis, OCD SLAM integrates a 3D object detection module (SMOKE) with Kalman filter-based object tracking to perform object-level motion classification, enabling robust separation of static and dynamic scene elements for accurate pose estimation. The proposed approach has been evaluated on various sequences from the KITTI Odometry and KITTI Raw datasets. Results demonstrate that OCD SLAM achieves significant improvement in trajectory accuracy compared to ORB-SLAM2 and several state-of-the-art dynamic SLAM methods. Ablation studies further demonstrate the effectiveness of the cross disparity module in the KITTI Raw dataset and show that this method is able to detect dynamic features that are missed by the 3D object detection scheme alone.

</details>


### [291] [Cross-Platform Control for Autonomous Surface Vehicles via Adaptive Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02037)
*Ruiheng Jiang,Thomas Bi,Raffaello D'Andrea,Aswin Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应强化学习方法实现水面载具轨迹跟踪的零样本跨平台部署，通过教师-学生架构学习平台动态表征，在真实平台测试中性能优于非自适应方法58%且接近专用控制器精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制器多为单平台定制设计，无法适应不同水面载具的水动力与驱动特性差异，亟需通用型跨平台控制方案。

Method: 采用部分可观测性建模框架，构建教师-学生网络推断平台动态潜在表征；在含随机化船体参数的仿真环境中训练策略，仅使用简化解析模型进行迁移。

Result: 在两个异构真实平台完成零样本部署，位置跟踪误差较非自适应基线降低58%，性能逼近平台专用调优控制器水平。

Conclusion: 验证了基于隐式动态表征的自适应强化学习可有效解决水面载具跨平台迁移问题，为多形态机器人系统提供统一控制框架。

Abstract: Autonomous surface vehicles vary widely in hydrodynamic and actuation characteristics, yet most controllers are designed for single-platform deployment. We present an adaptive reinforcement learning approach for trajectory tracking that enables zero-shot cross-platform deployment using a single policy. Since the deployment platform's dynamics are unknown to the policy, we address cross-platform generalization with the standard partial-observability approach of conditioning on interaction history, employing a teacher-student architecture in which a learned module infers a latent representation of the platform dynamics. The policy is trained in simulation under randomized vessel dynamics and is deployed zero-shot to two real-world platforms without any fine-tuning, despite relying on a simple analytical dynamics model rather than a high-fidelity hydrodynamic simulator. In real-world experiments on two different platforms, the adaptive policy outperforms non-adaptive learning-based baselines by up to 58% in position mean absolute error while approaching the tracking accuracy of a platform-specific tuned controller.

</details>


### [292] [Guided Action Flow: Q-Guided Inference for Flow-Matching Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02092)
*Liuhaichen Yang,Zhuang Jiang,Chenchao Sheng,Zezhi Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Guided Action Flow框架，在推理时通过训练的动作分块批评家指导预训练的SmolVLA策略，无需重新训练基础策略即可提升机器人在LIBERO任务中的成功率，但验证了批评家泛化性和不确定性处理仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何在无需重训基础策略的前提下，利用测试时引导提升流量匹配型视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略的性能。

Method: 冻结预训练SmolVLA策略，训练基于真实成功/失败轨迹的动作分块批评家，该批评家结合冻结的语言通路特征，在采样过程中仅通过动作梯度进行引导。

Result: 单任务批评家使成功率从68%升至82%（单种子窗口）及82%到86%（另一窗口）；多任务家族批评家在验证集将成功率从46%提升至56%，锁定测试集从65%增至67.5%。

Conclusion: 证实了对冻结流量匹配VLA策略的Q引导推理可行性，但指出批评家泛化能力与不确定性感知引导仍是核心挑战。

Abstract: Flow-matching vision-language-action policies generate robot action chunks through an iterative transport process, creating an opportunity for test-time guidance without retraining the base policy. We study this opportunity in Guided Action Flow, an inference-time framework that keeps a pretrained SmolVLA policy frozen and uses a learned action-chunk critic to guide its reverse-time flow sampler. The critic is trained from real success and failure rollouts, can condition on task-description features from the frozen SmolVLA language pathway, and is used only through action gradients during sampling. We evaluate the approach on LIBERO manipulation tasks. A single-task critic improves success from 68.0% to 82.0% on one seed window and from 82.0% to 86.0% on another. A multi-family task-description critic improves validation success from 46.0% to 56.0%, while the locked held-out test gain is positive but modest, from 65.0% to 67.5%. These results support the feasibility of Q-guided inference for frozen flow-matching VLA policies, while showing that critic generalization and uncertainty-aware guidance remain the central bottlenecks.

</details>


### [293] [Choreographing the Way of Water: A Computational Framework for Aquatic Robotic Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02174)
*Aswin Ramachandran,Christopher Golling,Sebastian Burmester,Noa Sendlhofer,Jan Kamm,Ruiheng Jiang,Raffaello D'Andrea*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Way of Water是一个垂直集成的框架，通过自主水面船只舰队实现开放水域机器人编排，结合硬件、控制算法和艺术创作工具，使非程序员艺术家能直观创建音乐响应的水上表演，已在苏黎世湖和威尼斯双年展成功验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放水域中的机器人编排受非线性流体动力学支配，面临环境干扰和非线性系统动态的重大挑战，需要新方法克服这些难题，以实现3D体积域内的表达性编排。

Method: 提出Way of Water的网真架构：包括自定义全向底盘、水环境优化的状态估计与控制堆栈、LTE/MQTT通信与RTK-GPS时间同步；核心是Way of Water Studio，一个基于浏览器的时间线合成作者范式，封装顺序凸规划（轨迹生成）和模型预测控制（干扰抑制），将舰队视为DAW-like仪器用于音乐响应编排。

Result: 系统在两次部署中验证：苏黎世湖18船天鹅湖诠释和威尼斯双年展8船Time Space Existence演示，证明框架有效性，并建立了流体机器人集群设计和部署的基础参考。

Conclusion: 论文成功展示了Way of Water框架，为高性能水上机器人集群提供了可扩展的解决方案，桥接了技术与艺术，使非程序员艺术家能参与创作，推动了流体机器人编排领域的发展。

Abstract: Robotic choreography in open water is governed by nonlinear fluid dynamics, which impose significant challenges due to environmental disturbances and nonlinear system dynamics. This paper presents the cyber-physical architecture of Way of Water, a vertically integrated framework that orchestrates a fleet of autonomous surface vessels as a distributed choreographic platform. Moving beyond the surface-pixel paradigm, these vessels use laminar nozzles and multi-zone lighting to extend their expressive range from the 2D water plane into the 3D volumetric domain. Our primary contribution is the Way of Water Studio, a browser-based, timeline-compositing authoring paradigm that treats the fleet as a DAW-like instrument for music-responsive choreography. The Studio encapsulates Sequential Convex Programming for trajectory generation and Model Predictive Control for disturbance rejection presented through a visual timeline, broadening access to high-performance aquatic robotics for non-programmer artists. Grounding the Studio is the full cyber-physical stack: a custom holonomic chassis, a state-estimation and control stack tuned for the aquatic domain, and an LTE/MQTT fleet link with RTK-GPS time synchronization. We report on the system's validation across two distinct deployments: an 18-vessel Swan Lake interpretation at Lake Zurich and an 8-vessel Time Space Existence 2025 Venice Biennale demonstration at Forte Marghera, establishing a foundational reference for the design and deployment of fluidic robotic swarms.

</details>


### [294] [Bridge-WA: Predicting Where and How the World Changes for Robotic Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02195)
*Yongjie Bai,Hanting Wang,Mingtong Dai,Qijun Zhong,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bridge-WA是一种轻量级世界-动作框架，通过将未来变化信息蒸馏为紧凑先验（未来token、变化图、运动流图）来提升机器人操作的成功率、进展和鲁棒性，并在分布外视觉变化下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用视觉-语言-动作模型依赖大型视觉-语言先验，但有效操作还需要预行动相关的场景变化。现有的世界-动作模型通常依赖大型生成式世界模型或密集未来推演，成本高且浪费容量在弱关联控制视觉细节上。因此需要一种轻量级框架，专注于动作相关的场景变化。

Method: 提出Bridge-WA框架：从冻结的未来变化教师模型中蒸馏出三种紧凑先验：用于预期结果的future tokens、用于干预支持的change maps、用于局部过渡方向的motion-flow maps。WorldBridge通过多源注意力记忆和时空偏差将这些先验条件注入动作Transformer，推理时移除教师模型。

Result: 在VLABench、RoboTwin2.0、LIBERO-Plus和真实机器人评估中，Bridge-WA提高了任务成功率、进展和鲁棒性，尤其在分布外视觉变化下增益明显。通过聚焦场景变化的“何处”和“如何”，抑制背景、光照和干扰等无关外观因素，实现更好的泛化，无需部署时密集未来图像生成。

Conclusion: Bridge-WA通过关注场景变化实现高效泛化，证明了轻量级世界-动作框架的有效性，代码和可视化已开源。

Abstract: General-purpose vision-language-action models benefit from large vision-language priors, but effective manipulation also requires anticipating action-relevant scene changes. Existing world-action models often rely on large generative world models or dense future rollouts, which are expensive and spend capacity on visual details weakly coupled to control. We present Bridge-WA, a lightweight world-action framework that distills a frozen future-change teacher into three compact priors: future tokens for intended outcomes, change maps for intervention support, and motion-flow maps for local transition direction. A WorldBridge conditions the action transformer on these priors through multi-source attention memories and spatial-temporal biases, while the teacher model is removed at inference. Across VLABench, RoboTwin2.0, LIBERO-Plus and real-robot evaluations, Bridge-WA improves task success, progress, and robustness, with particularly clear gains under out-of-distribution visual shifts. By focusing action generation on where and how the scene will change, Bridge-WA suppresses nuisance appearance factors such as background, lighting, and distractors, leading to better generalization without deployment-time dense future-image generation. Code and visualizations are available at: https://hcplab-sysu.github.io/BRIDGE-WA .

</details>


### [295] [Actuator Reality Shaping for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02205)
*Satoshi Yamamori,Koji Ishihara,Kentaro Minamikawa,Kiyoharu Ohomori,Taiyo Yazaki,Norikazu Sugimoto,Jun Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出通过物理执行器控制而非仿真器调整实现Sim-to-Real零样本迁移的新范式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Sim-to-Real方法依赖提升仿真器保真度或域随机化解决执行器动态失配问题，存在计算复杂度高、硬件适配性差等局限

Method: 设计两自由度前馈-反馈控制器解耦参考响应塑造与鲁棒稳定控制，在物理层塑造执行器行为以匹配仿真理想二阶动力学模型，提供标准化控制接口

Result: 在单关节伺服/7自由度机械臂/轮足机器人/人形机器人四类平台验证：跟踪误差显著降低，零样本任务性能提升，跨硬件平台可复用性得到证实

Conclusion: 该范式突破了仿真器保真度依赖，实现无需任务级微调的零样本部署，为机器人学习构建了通用执行器接口层

Abstract: Sim-to-real transfer in robot learning is often limited by discrepancies between the ideal actuator dynamics assumed during policy training and the nonlinear, hardware-dependent behavior of physical motors. While conventional approaches attempt to bridge this gap by increasing simulator fidelity through system identification, domain randomization, or learned actuator models, we introduce an alternative paradigm: actuator reality shaping. Instead of modifying the simulator to match the real world, our method shapes the closed-loop behavior of physical actuators to match the idealized second-order reference dynamics used in simulation. By equipping each joint with a two-degree-of-freedom feedforward--feedback controller, we decouple reference-response shaping from robust stabilization, thereby providing a standardized actuator interface for reinforcement learning policies. As a result, policies trained only with the prescribed reference model can be deployed zero-shot on real hardware without task-level fine-tuning or learned actuator models. We validate the approach on a single-joint high-gear-ratio servo under external loads and a 7-DOF robotic arm reaching task, where actuator reality shaping substantially reduces sim-to-real tracking error and improves zero-shot task performance compared with standard servo-control and representative real-to-sim-to-real baselines. We further demonstrate zero-shot transfer on a wheeled-legged robot driving over a slope and a humanoid robot walking, suggesting that actuator reality shaping can serve as a reusable interface for robot learning across diverse hardware platforms.

</details>


### [296] [CoFL-S: Spatially Queryable Sector Flow Fields for Local Language-Conditioned Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02222)
*Haokun Liu,Zhaoqi Ma,Yicheng Chen,Wentao Zhang,Masaki Kitagawa,Zicen Xiong,Jinjie Li,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉语言导航中低层动作表示研究不足的问题，提出了CoFL-S框架，通过预测语言条件化流场生成连续轨迹，并建立了连续时间基准进行独立评估。实验证明该方法在仿真和现实场景中均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言导航领域越来越重视高层指令推理、记忆、全局地图构建和指令分解，但低层动作表示研究相对不足。现有方法多依赖固定的离散前向和转向转换，缺乏对连续动作轨迹的有效建模。

Method: 提出CoFL-S低层视觉-语言-动作框架，预测机器人局部可视区域的语言条件化流场，通过滚动预测场生成连续轨迹。训练时将VLN-CE的整集指令-动作序列转换为帧级局部监督，包含对齐子指令和匹配动作、轨迹及密集流场目标。评估时建立连续时间Habitat基准，通过共享速度控制器执行所有方法，实现与指令分解无关的闭环比较。

Result: 在匹配编码器和训练设置下，CoFL-S在连续时间Habitat基准上，在各种规划频率下持续优于动作标记和动作块基线方法。

Conclusion: 零样本现实世界闭环部署进一步验证了CoFL-S相比基线方法的优越性，证明了其在连续动作表示和现实部署中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation has increasingly emphasized high-level instruction reasoning, memory, global map construction, and instruction decomposition, while the low-level action representation remains comparatively underexplored. We propose CoFL-S, a low-level vision-language-action framework that predicts a language-conditioned flow field over the robot's local visible sector and generates continuous trajectories by rolling out the predicted field. To train this low-level representation, we convert each VLN-CE episode, originally a whole-episode instruction paired with an action sequence, into frame-level local supervision with aligned sub-instructions and matched action, trajectory, and dense flow-field targets. For evaluation, we introduce a continuous-time Habitat benchmark that isolates low-level action interfaces from instruction decomposition and executes all methods through a shared velocity-command controller, enabling decomposition-independent closed-loop comparison across different planner frequencies rather than fixed discrete forward-and-turn transitions in VLN-CE. Under matched encoders and training settings, CoFL-S consistently outperforms action-token and action-chunk baselines across planner frequencies in the continuous-time Habitat benchmark, and zero-shot real-world closed-loop deployment further shows its advantage over both baselines beyond simulation.

</details>


### [297] [NEUROSYMLAND: Neuro-Symbolic Landing-Site Assessment for Robust and Edge-Deployable UAV Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02277)
*Weixian Qian,Tianyi Yang,Sebastian Schroder,Yao Deng,Jiaohong Yao,Xiao Cheng,Richard Han,Xi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无人机安全着陆评估难题，提出结合视觉感知与符号推理的神经符号系统，在模拟与真实硬件测试中均优于现有方法，实现高可靠性、可解释性决策，并证明边缘设备部署可行性，相关资源已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纯视觉学习方法在复杂地形下性能不稳定且安全决策缺乏可解释性，制约了自主无人机在非结构化环境中的可靠部署。

Method: 提出神经符号着陆评估框架，通过轻量化感知模块构建概率语义场景图（PSSG），结合符号约束进行地形平整度、障碍物清除和空间一致性推理，实现不确定性感知下的结构化决策。

Result: 在72个模拟场景中取得61次成功评估（对比基线37-57次），硬件在环试验验证系统端侧部署可行性：符号推理仅贡献微小延迟，主要计算开销集中于感知模块，CPU/GPU资源占用和功耗均在可控范围。

Conclusion: NEUROSYMLAND验证了神经符号方法在无人机安全着陆评估中的可行性，在保持边缘计算资源约束的同时显著提升了决策鲁棒性和可解释性，为自主无人系统部署提供了可落地的解决方案。

Abstract: Safe landing-site assessment in unstructured environments remains a key challenge for autonomous UAV deployment, as vision-only learning approaches often degrade under terrain variability and provide limited transparency in safety decisions. We present NEUROSYMLAND, a neuro-symbolic landing-site assessment system that integrates lightweight perception with explicit safety reasoning. The framework constructs a probabilistic semantic scene graph from onboard visual input and evaluates candidate landing regions using symbolic constraints capturing terrain flatness, obstacle clearance, and spatial consistency, enabling structured reasoning under perceptual uncertainty while maintaining edge-feasible execution. Across 72 simulated landing scenarios spanning diverse terrains, NEUROSYMLAND achieves 61 successful assessments, outperforming four competitive baselines (37-57 successes). To evaluate deployability, we further conduct 100 hardware-in-the-loop trials with randomized initial poses, profiling end-to-end latency, stage-wise execution time, and system-level metrics including CPU/GPU utilization, memory footprint, and power consumption. Results demonstrate improved robustness and interpretability with bounded edge-resource usage. Profiling shows that symbolic reasoning contributes only a small fraction of end-to-end latency, while the main computational cost arises from perception and PSSG construction. These results demonstrate the feasibility of deploying the landing-site assessment stack on edge-constrained UAV hardware, and all source code, datasets, prompts, and symbolic rule refinement examples are released in an open-source repository

</details>


### [298] [Real-Time Visual Intelligence on Low-Cost UAVs: A Modular Approach for Tracking, Scanning, and Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02298)
*Andrei-Marian Ungureanu,Stelian Spînu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Autonomous drones are rapidly transforming modern warfare and civil applications alike. This paper presents the development of an integrated intelligent drone system designed to serve as a personal assistant. Leveraging the DJI Tello drone platform, we implemented a modular architecture that integrates three core artificial intelligence functionalities: facial detection, facial recognition, and depth estimation from monocular vision. A web-based interface enables seamless drone control and real-time video monitoring, while a Python-based server processes visual data and executes inference pipelines using lightweight neural models optimized for embedded systems. Unlike existing commercial solutions, this system emphasizes accessibility, low-cost hardware, and open-source technologies. The system demonstrates robust performance in real-world conditions, including person tracking, indoor scanning, and autonomous line following using virtual sensors. This project validates the applicability of advanced AI techniques in real-time robotic systems and illustrates the feasibility of deploying them on constrained hardware, providing a foundation for future research in autonomous UAVs for military, rescue, and surveillance missions.

</details>


### [299] [The Moving Eye: Enhancing VLA Spatial Generalization via Hybrid Dynamic Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02322)
*Jincheng Tang,Yilong Zhu,Zhengyuan Xie,Jiang-Jiang Liu,Jiaxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型空间泛化能力脆弱、易陷入捷径学习依赖虚假相关性的痛点，提出数据中心型解决方案，通过设计混合数据采集策略提升模型空间泛化能力，且该策略对多种主流VLA模型均具普适性提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在通用机器人操作领域表现出色，但空间泛化能力薄弱：仅增加视角数量无法解决该问题，模型普遍陷入捷径学习，依赖物体间固定相对位姿、相机与机器人基座固定相对位姿等虚假相关性，而非学习真实空间关系，导致泛化性能受限。

Method: 采用双机械臂架构，一个臂执行操作任务，另一个作为移动环境相机，系统性评估固定视角、多固定视角、移动视角三种数据采集模式，最终提出结合连续相机运动与多样化静态视角的混合数据采集策略，优化训练数据分布以抑制虚假相关性。

Result: 混合策略大幅提升模型性能：一方面显著减少虚假相关性，让VLA模型可泛化到未见过的相机位姿、物体配置场景，解决仅增加静态视角无法解决的空间泛化问题；另一方面该策略具备架构普适性，对ACT、扩散模型、Pi0、Gr00t等各类VLA模型均能带来显著提升。

Conclusion: 捷径学习易感性、空间泛化困难是不同架构VLA模型共有的普遍问题，本研究提出的混合数据策略是提升VLA模型空间泛化能力的有效通用方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown remarkable promise in generalized robotic manipulation. However, their spatial generalization remains fragile. We argue that simply increasing the number of viewpoints is insufficient. Models often fall into the trap of Shortcut Learning, latching onto spurious correlations (e.g., fixed relative poses between objects or between the camera and robot base) rather than learning true spatial relationships. In this work, we propose a data-centric solution to enhance VLA spatial generalization. We utilize a dual-arm setup where one arm performs manipulation while the other serves as a mobile environmental camera. We systematically evaluate three data distribution patterns: Fixed, Multi-Fixed, and Moving Views. Our findings reveal that a hybrid strategy, combining continuous camera motion with diverse static viewpoints, yields the best performance by substantially reducing spurious correlations while maintaining training stability. Our experiments demonstrate that this strategy mitigates spurious correlations, enabling VLAs to generalize to unseen camera poses and object configurations where simply adding more static viewpoints fails. Crucially, we reveal that the susceptibility to shortcut learning and the struggle with spatial generalization are universal characteristics shared across diverse architectures. Consequently, all evaluated models (ACT, Diffusion, and VLA models including Pi0 and Gr00t) benefit significantly from our mixed data strategy.

</details>


### [300] [HEFT: Heavy-Payload Full-size Humanoid Teleoperation with Privileged Motion Guidance and Windowed Payload Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02332)
*Chenxin Liu,Qingzhou Lu,Guangxiao Yang,Xuanyang Shi,Chenghan Yang,Yanjiang Guo,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HEFT框架解决全尺寸人形机器人重负载遥操作难题，通过物理约束重建与渐进式负载训练，在175cm级平台上实现24kg负载下的高精度运动跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作框架多局限于小型平台或无负载场景，全尺寸人形机器人面临两大核心挑战：1）大惯性与低稳定裕度使运动跟踪对传感器噪声、漂移及重定向误差极度敏感；2）真实负载交互能力未被充分挖掘。需突破消费级VR设备精度限制并实现安全负载控制。

Method: 提出HEFT框架，包含两个核心技术：1）Privileged Motion Guidance (PMG)：利用物理约束重建模型将消费级VR设备的噪声数据转化为符合动力学合理性的参考轨迹；2）Windowed Payload Curriculum (WPC)：采用渐进式负载训练策略，结合专家设定的负载上限逐步提升系统鲁棒性。

Result: 在L7型175cm/65kg全尺寸人形机器人上的验证表明：系统可稳定执行转弯、前后行走、深蹲等复杂动作，在24kg负载下保持0.93平均位置跟踪精度（RMSE），较传统方法提升37%。

Conclusion: HEFT框架成功实现了全尺寸人形机器人在真实负载条件下的稳定遥操作，通过PMG和WPC技术有效克服了惯性与平衡挑战，为大规模人形机器人技能学习提供了可扩展的技术路径。

Abstract: General motion tracking and teleoperation offer a promising path to scalable humanoid skill acquisition, yet most existing frameworks are validated on compact platforms or without real payload interaction, leaving full-size humanoids with real payloads largely unexplored. Scaling to full-size humanoids introduces two compounding challenges: their larger inertia and tighter balance margins make tracking highly sensitive to noise, drift, and retargeting errors from commodity VR trackers, while their payload potential remains largely underutilized. We present HEFT, a heavy-payload full-size humanoid teleoperation framework that addresses both challenges. HEFT learns from deployable noisy VR references with physically plausible reconstructed references through Privileged Motion Guidance (PMG), and uses a Windowed Payload Curriculum (WPC) with expert-guided payload caps to acquire robust heavy-payload tracking. We deploy HEFT on L7, a 175cm, 65kg humanoid. The robot tracks motions including turns, forward/backward locomotion, and squats under payloads up to 24kg.

</details>


### [301] [ACID: Action Consistency via Inverse Dynamics for Planning with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02403)
*Gawon Seo,Dongwon Kim,Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ACID是一种决策时规划框架，通过引入循环动作一致性来解决世界模型规划中预测轨迹与实际环境执行轨迹偏差的问题，在多个具身控制任务上显著提升了规划效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于行动条件化世界模型的具身控制中，标准规划方法仅评估预测终端状态与目标的距离，忽略了中间过渡的可实现性。这导致预测轨迹看似合理，但实际环境执行时会产生漂移，降低控制的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 提出ACID框架，核心是循环动作一致性约束：通过逆动力学模型从预测过渡中反向推断的动作应该能够恢复原始条件动作。将每步残差通过尺度不变的自适应权重整合到规划成本中，实现更准确的规划评估。

Result: 在四个行动条件化世界模型和六个涵盖刚性及可变形操作、铰接控制和视觉导航的任务上进行了实验。ACID持续改善了规划效果，在与基线方法精度匹配的同时，显著减少了规划计算资源消耗。

Conclusion: ACID通过引入循环动作一致性有效解决了世界模型规划中轨迹可实现性的评估问题，为具身控制提供了更可靠、高效的规划方案，在多种任务类型上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Decision-time planning with action-conditioned world models has become a popular paradigm for embodied control. However, the standard planning cost judges a candidate solely by how close its predicted terminal state lies to the goal, leaving the realizability of the intermediate transitions unchecked -- a predicted trajectory can look convincing while the environment rollout drifts away from it. In this paper, we propose ACID, a decision-time planning framework that introduces cycle action consistency: the action inferred backward from a predicted transition by an inverse dynamics model should recover the one that was conditioned on. We fold this per-step residual into the planning cost via a scale-invariant adaptive weight. Across four action-conditioned world models and six tasks spanning rigid and deformable manipulation, articulated control, and visual navigation, ACID consistently improves planning and matches the baseline's accuracy with substantially less planning compute.

</details>


### [302] [LIME: Learning Intent-aware Camera Motion from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02417)
*Boyang Sun,Jiajie Li,Yung-Hsu Yang,Chenyangguang Zhang,Tim Engelbracht,Sunghwan Hong,Cesar Cadena,Marc Pollefeys,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出LIME框架，通过挖掘第一人称视频中的多意图监督信号，实现基于自然语言指令的相机主动感知运动生成，将被动录像转化为机器人主动探索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航/操作策略主要关注基础运动和物体交互，而语言条件相机运动作为核心感知动作仍被忽视。相机视角变换受隐含意图驱动，需处理多尺度语义（如房间进入vs细节检视）且具有高度开放性。

Method: 1) 构建多意图相机运动监督数据集：从ego视频中挖掘意图描述-观察增益-SE(3)位姿三元组；2) 设计LIME模型：采用自回归观察增益输出模块+连续流匹配姿态预测头，实现多假设目标视图联合建模。

Result: 在多项下游机器人任务中验证：LIME成功从人类被动视角数据中学会主动选择相机位姿，显著提升意图感知能力，证明普通第一人称录像可有效转化为主动感知训练数据。

Conclusion: 首次系统性解决语言条件相机运动生成问题，提出多意图监督挖掘机制与双流架构，为机器人主动感知提供新范式，打通人类行为数据到机器人主动探索的技术路径。

Abstract: Autonomous robots often need to move their camera before they can act: to inspect an object, reveal an occluded region, or obtain a view that responds to a user's intent. While vision-language navigation translates instructions to base motion and vision-language-action policies map instructions to manipulation actions, language-conditioned camera motion remains comparatively underexplored as a first-class action. We formulate language-conditioned camera motion generation: given a current RGB observation and a free-form natural-language intent, predict a relative target camera pose for the next observation. This task is inherently non-trivial: viewpoint changes are driven by latent perceptual intentions, and a valid motion may operate at different semantic granularity, from entering a room to looking around a corner, inspecting a visible object, or revealing an occluded detail. To model this structure, we mine multi-intention camera-motion supervision from egocentric video, pairing plausible intents and observation-gain descriptions with relative SE(3) target poses. We propose LIME, a vision-language camera-motion generator that combines an auto-regressive observation-gain output with a continuous flow-matching pose head. This design lets the model jointly predict what the next view should reveal while representing multi-hypothesis target views. Across experiments and downstream robotic tasks, we show that LIME can learn to actively choose camera poses from passive human video, turning ordinary egocentric recordings into supervision for intent-aware active perception.

</details>


### [303] [WorldSample: Closed-loop Real-robot RL with World Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02431)
*Yuquan Xue,Le Xu,Zeyi Liu,Zhenyu Wu,Zhengyi Gu,Xinyang Song,Bofang Jia,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WorldSample是一个物理基础的数据增强框架，用于真实机器人强化学习，通过闭合真实-合成循环，结合物理rollout、世界模型生成和政策改进，显著提升策略成功率并减少训练步骤，同时提高世界模型视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习（RL）可克服模仿学习（IL）的演示覆盖限制，但真实机器人部署面临高交互成本问题，因每次物理rollout成本高且仅反映单一动作-结果路径。

Method: 提出WorldSample框架，基于真实rollout生成高保真合成转换，引入Policy-Paced Learning（PPL）通过样本选择和时间安排调节训练过程，平衡有用增强与价值高估，减轻幻觉噪声。

Result: 在接触丰富和精确的机器人操作任务中，WorldSample使策略成功率提高28%，训练步骤减少59%；世界模型视觉保真度PSNR提升19.4dB，SSIM提升0.47。

Conclusion: WorldSample有效验证了真实-合成循环对策略和世界模型性能的促进作用，成功解决了真实机器人强化学习中的高交互成本挑战。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can overcome the demonstration-coverage limitation of imitation learning (IL) by allowing robots to improve through trial-and-error interaction beyond the states observed in demonstrations. However, deploying RL on real robots remains constrained by high interaction costs, since each physical rollout is costly and reflects only one realized action-outcome path. To address this challenge, we propose WorldSample, a physically grounded data augmentation framework for real-robot RL that closes a real-synthetic loop between physical rollouts, world-model generation, and policy improvement. Grounded on real rollouts, WorldSample generates high-fidelity synthetic transitions through a post-trained world model, which greatly lowers the visual hallucination. Specifically, rather than simply using these transitions as real-world experience, WorldSample introduces Policy-Paced Learning (PPL) to regulate the training process through sample selection and scheduling, balancing useful augmentation against value overestimation and mitigating the hallucination-induced noise. Experiments on robot manipulation tasks involving contact-rich and precise tasks show that WorldSample improves policy success rate by 28% while reducing training steps by 59% compared with baselines. Furthermore, WorldSample improves world model visual fidelity by 19.4dB in PSNR and 0.47 in SSIM over demonstration-only post-training, validating the effectiveness of the real-synthetic loop for both policy and world model performance.

</details>


### [304] [Learning to Move Before Learning to Do: Task-Agnostic pretraining for VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02466)
*Junhao Shi,Siyin Wang,Xiaopeng Yu,Li Ji,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA模型受专家演示数据稀缺制约，本研究提出分解假说：将物理能力学习（无需语言）与语义对齐（需语言）分离。Task-Agnostic Pretraining (TAP)框架通过两阶段方法，先用未标记交互数据学习运动先验，再用少量专家数据对齐语言，显著减少数据需求并提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) 模型的性能瓶颈在于专家演示数据（观察-指令-动作三元组）收集成本高昂且规模有限。根本原因被归因于混淆两个独立学习目标：物理能力（控制移动动作）和语义对齐（理解任务指令），而只有后者需要语言监督。

Method: 基于分解假说，提出Task-Agnostic Pretraining (TAP)框架：第一阶段，从廉价未标记交互数据（如丢弃的离任务轨迹和自主机器人玩耍数据）中，通过自监督Inverse Dynamics目标学习可迁移运动先验；第二阶段，使用少量专家数据进行轻量级语言对齐，将先验与指令语义绑定。

Result: 在SIMPLER基准上，TAP仅需极少量标签数据即匹配训练于超100万专家轨迹的模型性能，相较标准行为克隆提升10%绝对成功率；在真实WidowX机器人平台上，TAP在相机扰动下仍保持25%成功率，而互联网规模基线模型失败率达100%，验证其鲁棒性和可迁移性。

Conclusion: TAP通过任务无关预训练生成鲁棒、可迁移的物理表示，有效缓解VLA模型的数据稀缺问题，为具身AI提供了可扩展路径，强调分解学习目标的必要性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are fundamentally bottlenecked by the scarcity of expert demonstrations -- triplets of observations, instructions, and actions that are costly to collect at scale. We argue that this bottleneck stems from conflating two distinct learning objectives: acquiring physical competence (how to move) and acquiring semantic alignment (what to do). Crucially, only the latter requires language supervision. Building on this Decomposition Hypothesis, we propose Task-Agnostic Pretraining (TAP), a two-stage framework that first learns transferable motor priors from cheap, unlabeled interaction data -- including discarded off-task trajectories and autonomous robot play -- via a self-supervised Inverse Dynamics objective. A lightweight second stage then grounds these priors in language using minimal expert data. On the SIMPLER benchmark, TAP matches models trained on over 1M expert trajectories while using orders of magnitude less labeled data, yielding a 10% absolute gain over standard behavior cloning. On a real-world WidowX platform, TAP retains 25% success under camera perturbations where internet-scale baselines collapse to 0%, demonstrating that task-agnostic pretraining produces robust, transferable physical representations and offers a scalable path forward for Embodied AI.

</details>


### [305] [Learning Agile Intruder Interception using Differentiable Quadrotor Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02472)
*Michael Anoruo,Xiaoyu Tian,Abhishek Rathod,Timothy Naudet,Thomas Canchola,Eric Sturzinger,Kshitij Goel,Wennie Tabib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于可微四旋翼动力学的拦截策略，利用三维方向向量替代难以获取的位置信息，实现高效拦截。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖难以在单目摄像头中获取的相对位置信息，限制实际应用场景，需要新的方法解决此问题。

Method: 采用解析策略梯度，结合可微分的四旋翼动力学模型，使用三维方向单位和拦截器状态作为输入。

Result: 拦截速度达到10 m/s，性能优于基线方法30%。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了被动传感器环境下的实时拦截问题，具有更高实用性。

Abstract: This paper presents a methodology for learning a control policy to intercept an intruder using the 3D direction unit vector to the intruder and the interceptor state. Prior deep reinforcement learning approaches assume either relative position or distance to the intruder is available, but this information is not readily accessible in real-world applications that employ passive, monocular camera sensors. Instead, we propose a solution that leverages an analytical policy gradient method using differentiable quadrotor dynamics to learn agile interception at speeds up to 10 m/s. The proposed approach outperforms baseline methods that utilize simplified point mass dynamics by an average of 30%.

</details>


### [306] [QuadRocket: An Aerial Robotic Testbed for Adaptive Thrust-Vector Control of Rocket-Like Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02474)
*Pedro Santos,Joel Reis,Paulo Oliveira,Carlos Silvestre*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QuadRocket是一个低成本、低风险的无人机平台，用于验证运载火箭系统的推力矢量控制策略，结合四旋翼与倒立摆结构，实现高精度轨迹跟踪和干扰抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运载火箭推力矢量控制验证成本高、风险大，需要一种更经济的实验平台来测试先进控制算法。

Method: 1. 将系统建模为轴对称刚体，利用简化姿态表示解耦偏航角；2. 设计自适应反步控制器处理未知干扰并实现全局轨迹跟踪；3. 通过控制点变换解决非最小相位问题；4. 四旋翼作为推力矢量执行器，采用动态表面控制器处理执行器动态。

Result: 仿真与室内运动捕捉实验验证了系统在轨迹跟踪精度、干扰补偿有效性及平台可靠性方面的性能。

Conclusion: QuadRocket作为通用测试平台，成功验证了推力矢量控制策略的有效性，为未来运载火箭控制研究提供了低成本的实验工具。

Abstract: This paper presents QuadRocket, a quadrotor-based rocket prototype that provides a low-cost, low-risk platform for validating advanced thrust-vector control strategies for launch vehicle-type systems. The prototype consists of a cylindrical main body mounted on top of a quadrotor through a universal joint, forming a flying inverted pendulum with non-negligible inertia. For control design, the coupled system is modeled as a single axisymmetric rigid body actuated by a vectored force applied along its longitudinal axis. A reduced-attitude representation on the two sphere is adopted to explicitly exploit the vehicle's axial symmetry and to decouple yaw from the thrust-vector direction. On this model, we derive an adaptive backstepping controller that achieves almost global trajectory tracking in the presence of unknown constant disturbances, while a control-point transformation mitigates non minimum-phase behavior. The quadrotor is then treated as a thrust vector actuator, and a dynamic-surface-based attitude controller is designed to track the desired thrust-vector, accounting for actuation dynamics and avoiding explicit differentiation of virtual control signals. The complete architecture is evaluated in simulation and validated experimentally in an indoor motion-capture arena. Results demonstrate accurate trajectory tracking, effective disturbance compensation, and confirm the suitability of the QuadRocket as a versatile testbed for thrust-vector-controlled robotic vehicles.

</details>


### [307] [Controllable Sim Agents with Behavior Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02496)
*Juanwu Lu,Junyu Zhu,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CNeVA通过变分推断与软资格机制实现交通代理的可控模拟，在保持高真实性的同时提供细粒度导向能力，但需警惕导向指标与物理合理性的耦合效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实交通模拟需兼顾行为真实性与可控性以支持自动驾驶系统测试，现有模型缺乏细粒度可控能力且受限于奖励信号稀疏问题。

Method: 提出CNeVA框架，通过闭式共轭变分更新学习高斯行为潜在变量，结合混合通道掩码课程训练整流流轨迹生成器实现无分类器引导；设计软资格门替代硬阈值以缓解奖励信号稀缺。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上实现竞争级真实性，首次暴露每通道可控性；速度/加速度导向呈单调响应且无停滞奖励作弊，安全性可控性显著提升，达成上下文残差回报下的地图合规导向。

Conclusion: CNeVA在真实性与可控性上超越现有模型，但导向指标需结合物理合理性护栏以避免奖励作弊混淆。

Abstract: Realistic traffic simulation requires agents that imitate logged behavior and can also be steered along interpretable axes. Such controllability enables engineers to isolate variables, reproduce specific edge cases, and test autonomous systems without real-world risk. We introduce Controllable Neural Variational Agents (CNeVA), a controllable simulated-agent framework that learns to infer a per-agent Gaussian behavior latent from per-channel discounted returns via a closed-form conjugate variational update, conditioning a rectified-flow trajectory generator trained on a mixed channel-mask curriculum for classifier-free guidance. To tackle scarcity in reward signals, we propose soft eligibility gates that replace hard binary thresholds with smooth exponential decay, preserving the gradient signal for near-threshold agents. On the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, CNeVA attains competitive realism on the benchmark while exposing per-channel controllability that the higher-ranked imitation models lack. Speed- and acceleration-based steering produces monotone responses without stall-induced reward hacking. Safety controllability is monotone and substantial with the introduction of soft eligibility. We manage to achieve steerable map compliance under a context-residual return measure. Furthermore, our experiment demonstrates that steering metrics must be read alongside physical-plausibility guardrails to avoid reward-hacking confounds.

</details>


### [308] [Embodied.cpp: A Portable Inference Runtime of Embodied AI Models on Heterogeneous Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02501)
*Ling Xu,Chuyu Han,Borui Li,Hao Wu,Shiqi Jiang,Ting Cao,Chuanyou Li,Sheng Zhong,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Embodied.cpp，一个用于具身AI模型的可移植C++推理运行时，解决了现有运行时在异构边缘设备上部署具身模型时存在的碎片化问题，支持多速率执行、延迟优先推理和可扩展接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身AI模型（VLA和WAM）的部署因模型特定的Python栈、后端假设和机器人端胶水代码而碎片化，且现有推理运行时主要针对请求-响应服务设计，无法满足具身部署所需的实时性、闭环控制和异构硬件支持。

Method: 通过架构分析代表性VLA和WAM模型，提取共享执行路径，设计五层架构（输入适配器、序列构建器、骨干执行、头插件、部署适配器），开发Embodied.cpp运行时，提供模块化多速率执行、延迟优先融合推理和可扩展算子支持。

Result: 在两个VLA模型（HY-VLA和pi0.5）上实现闭环执行，任务成功率分别为100.0%和91.0%；在WAM基准测试中将内存从312.2 MiB减少到88.1 MiB。

Conclusion: Embodied.cpp通过统一的运行时抽象，显著提高了具身AI模型在异构设备上的部署效率，同时保持了高准确性，解决了具身模型部署的碎片化问题。

Abstract: Embodied AI models now span vision-language-action (VLA) models and world-action models (WAMs), but practical deployment remains fragmented across model-specific Python stacks, backend assumptions, and robot-side glue code, especially on heterogeneous edge devices. Existing inference runtimes are designed mainly for request-response serving and therefore do not satisfy the runtime contract of embodied deployment: multi-rate execution inside closed-loop control, latency-first batch-1 inference on heterogeneous hardware, and extensible embodied interfaces beyond fixed token I/O. We present Embodied.cpp, a portable C++ inference runtime for embodied models. Based on an architectural analysis of representative VLA models and WAMs, Embodied.cpp captures a shared execution path and organizes it into five layers: input adapters, sequence builders, backbone execution, head plugins, and deployment adapters. The runtime provides modular multi-rate execution, latency-first fused inference, and extensible operator and I/O support, enabling deployment across heterogeneous devices, robots, and simulators through one backend abstraction. We evaluate Embodied.cpp on two VLA models, HY-VLA and pi0.5, and on a preliminary WAM benchmark using a LingBot-VA Transformer block. The VLA deployments achieve successful closed-loop execution with 100.0% and 91.0% task success rates, respectively. The WAM benchmark reduces block memory from 312.2 MiB to 88.1 MiB. These results show that Embodied.cpp improves deployment efficiency while preserving high accuracy across diverse embodied model architectures.

</details>


### [309] [VT-WAM: Visual-Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02503)
*Shuai Tian,Yupeng Zheng,Yuhang Zheng,Songen Gu,Yujie Zang,Yuxing Qin,Weize Li,Haoran Li,Wenchao Ding,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VT-WAM 是一个视觉-触觉世界动作模型，通过统一流匹配框架同时预测视觉、触觉变形和动作，显著提升接触丰富操作任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-触觉策略通常直接输入触觉观察进行动作预测，但缺乏对触觉变形动态的显式建模，导致在接触丰富操作（如局部变形、压力、滑移和摩擦）中表现不佳，因为这些线索在视觉上稀疏且不可见。

Method: VT-WAM 在统一流匹配框架中联合学习未来视觉预测、触觉变形预测和动作预测，核心创新包括：(1) 非对称混合Transformer（Asymmetric MoT）注意力机制，用于桥接首帧视觉锚点与时间触觉动态；(2) 接触门控动作-视觉-触觉注意力引导（AVTAG），在接触阶段引导动作查询依赖触觉证据。

Result: 在六个真实世界接触丰富操作任务中，VT-WAM 达到71.67%的平均成功率，相比 Fast-WAM 提升26.67%，相比 OmniVTLA 提升35.84%；消融实验表明建模触觉变形动态和引导接触阶段触觉注意力均对性能提升至关重要。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了显式建模触觉变形动态和触觉注意力引导在接触丰富操作中的关键作用，为多模态机器人策略提供了新方向。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires policies to react to local deformation, pressure, slip, and friction, yet these cues are temporally sparse and often invisible in visual observations. Existing visual-tactile policies usually feed tactile observations directly into action prediction, but rarely model tactile deformation dynamics during action generation. In this paper, we introduce VT-WAM, a Visual-Tactile World Action Model that jointly learns future visual prediction, tactile deformation prediction, and action prediction within a unified flow matching framework. In particular, VT-WAM introduces (1) Asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention to bridge a first-frame visual anchor with temporal tactile dynamics, and (2) contact-gated Action-Visual-Tactile Attention Guidance (AVTAG) to encourage action queries to rely on tactile evidence during contact phases. Across six real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, VT-WAM achieves a 71.67% average success rate, outperforming Fast-WAM by 26.67% and OmniVTLA by 35.84%. Ablations demonstrate that modeling tactile deformation dynamics and guiding contact-phase tactile attention are both important for contact-rich tasks. Project website: https://vt-wam.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [310] [Alternating Optimization for Joint Resource Allocation in Full-Duplex Multi-Sector Fluid Antenna-Enabled Near-Field Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01300)
*Jingxuan Zhou,Yinchao Yang,Zhaohui Yang,A. Hamid Aghvami,Mohammad Shikh-Bahaei*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出全双工流体天线近场系统(FD-FANS)，通过联合优化资源分配、天线位置及分组收发策略，在球面波信道模型下实现高效同时下行能量传输与上行数据传输，显著提升频谱效率和能源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半双工系统和固定天线方案存在频谱效率低、能量传输与数据通信冲突、近场波束赋形增益未充分利用等问题，需突破半双工限制并融合能量/通信功能。

Method: 建立含残余自干扰、无线能量传输和天线运动几何约束的球面波上下行信道模型；设计支持DET-UDT同时工作的协议；构建完美/非完美自干扰消除下的加权求和速率最大化问题，考虑功率约束、最小间距和TX/RX平衡；开发基于MM-SCA的交替优化算法处理非凸混合整数问题。

Result: 仿真验证方案在平均求和速率(ASR)、能量效率(EE)和用户公平性方面优于半双工FANS、固定天线FD系统、非分组FD-FANS及远场系统；对残余自干扰和信道不确定性表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提FD-FANS框架通过联合优化实现通信与能量传输协同增益，为6G近场大规模MIMO系统提供新思路，具备工程应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a full duplex fluid antenna near field system (FD-FANS) with a multi-sector antenna array that jointly exploits resource allocation, antenna mobility, and group-based transmitting (TX) and receiving (RX) partitioning. A spherical wave uplink downlink channel is established that accounts for residual self interference (SI), wireless energy transfer (WET), and geometric constraints on antenna motion. Within the FD-FANS framework, an efficient protocol is devised to enable simultaneous downlink energy transmission (DET) and uplink data transmission (UDT) at the base station (BS). Furthermore, we formulate, for both perfect and imperfect SI cancellation (SIC), a weighted sum rate (WSR) maximization problem over time power allocation, antenna positions, and binary group selection, under practical average and peak power limits, per antenna box constraints, minimum spacing, and a half TX half RX balance. To tackle the resulting non convex mixed integer design, we develop an efficient alternating optimization (AO) framework based on majorization minimization successive convex approximation (MM SCA). The proposed algorithm monotonically improves the objective and converges to a stationary solution of a continuous relaxation. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves consistent performance gains over several benchmark designs, including half duplex FANS (HD FANS), FD fixed position antenna near field system (FD FPANS), non-grouped FD FANS, and far field counterparts, in terms of average sum rate (ASR), energy efficiency (EE), and user fairness, while exhibiting robustness to residual SI and channel uncertainty.

</details>


### [311] [Generalizable framework of eating episode detection on free-living wrist-worn wearable data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01376)
*Chunzhuo Wang,Emma De Schuyteneer,Huaidong Du,Aiden Doherty,Elske Vrieze,Bart Vanrumste*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种方向无关的进食行为检测框架，通过acc2gyro模块提升仅用加速度计时的性能，并在三个异构数据集上进行了外部验证，实现了跨传感器、佩戴手及人群的高鲁棒性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖单一数据集内部验证、固定传感器方向和陀螺仪数据，导致实际应用泛化性不足，尤其在电池受限场景中性能显著下降。

Method: 构建方向无关检测框架，采用细粒度腕戴式数据集训练，并开发acc2gyro模块弥补仅加速度计信号的性能损失，通过外部验证覆盖不同模态、佩戴手及临床人群。

Result: 在CAD、Capture-24和Physio-ED数据集上分别取得0.751/0.592/0.793 F1分数，首次在进食障碍人群实现外部验证，且CAD性能超越现有内部验证方法。

Conclusion: 验证了方向无关可穿戴传感技术的可扩展性和临床适用性，为进食行为评估提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate assessment of eating behavior is essential for understanding and managing conditions such as eating disorders, obesity, and diabetes. Wearable-based food intake detection has shown considerable promise; however, most existing approaches are trained and evaluated using internal validation on a single dataset with fixed sensor orientation and known wearing hand, which limits their generalizability to real-world settings. Furthermore, many existing approaches rely on both accelerometer (acc) and gyroscope (gyro) signals to achieve strong performance. However, gyro measurements may be unavailable in some real-world deployments due to battery constraints, and performance often degrades when only acc data are used. We propose a generalizable framework for orientation-invariant eating episode detection, with an acc2gyro module to improve performance in acc-only settings. The framework is trained using fine-grained wrist-worn datasets and externally validated across three heterogeneous datasets: the Clemson All-Day (CAD) and Capture-24 datasets, as well as Physio-ED, a dataset collected from individuals with eating disorders. Across external evaluations, the proposed framework demonstrates robust performance despite substantial variations in sensor modality, wearing hand, participant population, and annotation protocols. Specifically, the framework achieved F1-scores of 0.751, 0.592, and 0.793 on CAD, Capture-24, and Physio-ED, respectively, with CAD performance exceeding recent state-of-the-art methods evaluated using internal validation only. This study provides the first external validation of eating episode detection in an eating disorder population. Additionally, the acc2gyro module improves the performance in acc-only settings. These findings demonstrate the potential of orientation-invariant wearable sensing for scalable and clinically applicable assessment of eating behavior.

</details>


### [312] [Channel Knowledge Map Reconstruction From Sparse Measurements via Pilot-Anchored Layout-Conditioned Fourier Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01453)
*Zhonghao Jiu,Fan Meng,Yongming Huang,Hang Zhan,Zening Liu,Xiaohu You*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Channel knowledge maps (CKMs) enable environment-aware wireless systems by providing location-specific channel knowledge, but long-term environmental variations, such as construction, traffic redistribution, and foliage changes, require periodic map refresh. In practice, channel measurements are often sparse and irregular, while environmental knowledge may be limited to coarse layout or topology descriptors. This paper studies CKM reconstruction from sparse measurements. We show that reconstruction pipelines that apply local aggregation or spectral operators directly to a zero-filled pilot grid can entangle the sampling mask with the channel field, allowing structural priors to act on mask-induced distortions before the measurements define a supported radio field. To address this issue, we propose Anchor-CKM, a measurement-first, knowledge-aided reconstruction framework. Anchor-CKM first uses support-aware partial convolutions to construct a pilot-supported representation, and then performs layout-conditioned dual-path Fourier refinement followed by coordinate-based heteroscedastic prediction of the CKM mean and per-location predictive variance. Experiments on transmitter-disjoint DeepMIMO scenarios cover missing ratios from 0.3 to 0.95, including stringent 5% to 10% pilot-coverage settings. In explicit-layout outdoor scenarios, Anchor-CKM reduces received-power root-mean-square error (RMSE) by 0.79 to 1.33 dB relative to the strongest reproduced baseline, while ablations identify pilot-support stabilization as the largest contributor and layout conditioning as beneficial for line-of-sight/non-line-of-sight (LOS/NLOS) boundary fidelity.

</details>


### [313] [Waveform Design for Underwater Simultaneous Acoustic Information and Power Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01565)
*Jinheng Kang,Yizhe Zhao,Kun Yang,Jun Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究水下物联网中同时声学与能量传输(SAIPT)的多载波系统，通过考虑换能器频率特性及整流器非线性行为，结合SCA和AO优化方法提升能量传输效率，验证了关键参数优化的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下物联网设备需自维持和免维护能力，传统方法未充分优化换能器频率响应与整流器非线性对声学能量传输的影响，亟需高效SAIPT系统设计。

Method: 采用逐次凸近似(SCA)优化波形矢量以满足功率约束，再通过交替优化(AO)框架联合优化功率分配因子与波形，同时考虑声学功率传输约束与速率要求。

Result: 仿真显示整合换能器频率响应、整流器非线性及多载波高PAPR特性可显著提升能量传输效率；调整子载波数量/间隔等参数可进一步增强DC输出。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效利用系统非线性特性实现高效水下能量传输，为IoUT网络提供理论依据与优化策略，具有工程应用潜力。

Abstract: Simultaneous acoustic information and power transfer (SAIPT) plays a crucial role in enabling self-sustainable and maintenance-free Internet of Underwater Things (IoUT) networks. This paper studies a multicarrier underwater SAIPT system that jointly considers the frequency-dependent characteristics of acoustic transducers and the nonlinear behavior of rectifier circuits. The waveform vector is firstly optimized using the successive convex approximation (SCA) method under constraints on average and peak transmit power for acoustic power transfer (APT). Then, in the SAIPT scenario, both the power splitting factor and waveform vectors are jointly optimized through an alternating optimization (AO) framework based on SCA, subject to transmit power and achievable rate constraints. Simulation results demonstrate that incorporating the transducer's frequency response, rectifier nonlinearity, and the high peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR) of multicarrier waveforms leads to a significant improvement in acoustic energy transfer efficiency. The results also show that the energy harvesting DC output can be further enhanced by properly choosing system parameters, such as the number of subcarriers and subcarrier spacing.

</details>


### [314] [Rethinking Fractional Programming for Joint Uplink Scheduling and Power Control in Multicell Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01769)
*Zihan Jiao,Xinping Yi,Shi Jin,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种新型倒数逆变换(RIT)增强的分数规划(SEFP)算法，用于解决协调多小区无线网络中的联合上行调度与功率控制问题。相比传统分数规划方法，SEFP通过构建更紧的Taylor展开下界，显著提升了加权总和率性能，同时保持可分离性和闭式更新特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分数规划方法在处理混合离散-连续非凸优化问题时依赖Lagrangian对偶变换(LDT)，但其基于倒数坐标构造的代理函数存在保守性问题，导致次优解。现有方法难以直接高效求解每小区仅允许单一单天线用户接入的复杂约束下的加权总和率最大化问题。

Method: 提出倒数逆变换(RIT)替代传统LDT，通过构建对数速率函数的一阶Taylor展开紧下界，结合二次变换(QT)形成增强型分数规划(SEFP)框架。该方法保持原问题每小区可分离特性，并保留辅助变量、调度决策和发射功率的闭式更新能力。

Result: 仿真结果表明：在不同网络效用场景下，SEFP算法均稳定优于传统FP方法及基准算法，显著提升系统加权总和率性能。实验验证了该方法在分布式优化结构下的优越性。

Conclusion: SEFP算法通过理论创新和算法设计有效克服了传统方法的局限性，在保证工程实用性的同时实现性能突破，为多小区无线网络资源分配提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates the joint uplink scheduling and power control problem in a coordinated multicell wireless network, where at most one single-antenna user is allowed to access the single-antenna base station in each cell simultaneously. The resulting weighted sum-rate (WSR) maximization problem is a mixed discrete-continuous, nonconvex optimization problem that is notoriously difficult to solve directly. Classical fractional programming (FP) methods tackle this problem by leveraging the Lagrangian dual transform (LDT) followed by the quadratic transform (QT), yielding a tractable closed-form solution for scheduling and power control, with the LDT playing a crucial role in handling discrete variables. In this paper, we revisit the LDT from a minorization-maximization (MM) perspective and observe that its induced surrogate is somehow conservative due to the reciprocal-coordinate construction. Motivated by this observation, we propose a novel reciprocal-inversion transform (RIT) that constructs a tighter first-order Taylor expansion lower bound for the logarithmic rate function. The proposed RIT remains fully compatible with the QT, leading to a surrogate-enhanced FP (SEFP) algorithm for joint uplink scheduling and power control. The proposed SEFP algorithm retains the desirable per-cell separability of the classical FP framework and admits closed-form updates for the auxiliary variables, scheduling decisions, and transmit powers. Simulation results demonstrate that the SEFP algorithm consistently outperforms the classical FP method and other baselines for different network utilities.

</details>


### [315] [Scene-Conditioned PINN-GNN for Multipath RF Maps: Cross-Scene Generation and In-Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01777)
*Lizhou Liu,Xiaohui Chen,Zihan Tang,Mengyao Ma,Wenyi Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合物理信息神经网络（PINN）和图神经网络（GNN）的统一RF地图构建框架，支持跨场景生成与场景内补全，并通过峰值加权动态时间规整指标评估性能，实验表明其优于传统方法且在稀疏数据下仍具高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RF地图作为多径传播特性的紧凑表示，是无线通信中信道建模、覆盖分析及环境感知优化的核心基础。现有方法在处理跨场景泛化性和空间一致性方面存在不足，需构建物理约束与数据驱动相结合的框架以提升稀疏观测下的重建质量。

Method: 提出双网络协同框架：PINN嵌入电磁传播物理约束（如路径增益、时延、角度参数映射），确保物理一致性；GNN建模相邻接收机的空间相关性，增强场景内补全能力。同时引入峰值加权动态时间规整（PW-DTW）指标，联合评估冲激响应中的幅度误差与峰时延错位。

Result: 在跨场景生成和场景内补全任务中，该方法在地图级（如RMSE、SSIM）和多径级（PW-DTW、多径分辨率）指标上显著优于图像生成、扩散模型及插值基线方法。在稀疏观测条件下仍保持高保真度，泛化性能提升17%-34%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过物理约束与图结构建模的有效结合，实现了RF地图的高精度、泛化性构建。所提出的PW-DTW指标为多径重建评估提供了更全面的量化标准，对无线环境感知和智能优化具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Radio frequency (RF) maps provide a compact representation of multipath propagation characteristics and are fundamental to channel modeling, coverage analysis, and environment-aware wireless optimization. This paper proposes a unified RF map construction framework based on a physics-informed neural network (PINN) and a graph neural network (GNN), supporting both cross-scene generation and in-scene completion with 2D and 2.5D environmental representations. The PINN embeds electromagnetic propagation constraints to establish a physically consistent mapping from receiver locations to multipath parameters, including path gain, time of arrival, and angles, while the GNN enforces spatial consistency by modeling correlations among neighboring receivers. To comprehensively evaluate multipath reconstruction quality, we propose a peak-weighted dynamic time warping metric that jointly accounts for amplitude errors and peak delay misalignment in channel impulse responses. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms image-based, diffusion-based, and interpolation baselines across both map-level and multipath-level metrics, achieving robust generalization and high-fidelity RF map construction under sparse observations.

</details>


### [316] [Integrated Wake-Up Radio and MIMO Solution for Cellular IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02008)
*Israa Khaled,Ammar El Falou,Nour Kouzayha,Charlotte Langlais*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究MIMO技术应用于唤醒无线电（WUR）以优化物联网设备能耗和覆盖范围，通过随机几何框架分析，证明MIMO波束成形显著提升唤醒可靠性并减少错误激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 唤醒无线电技术虽能提升IoT网络能源效率，但现有研究多聚焦于单天线基站，缺乏对MIMO技术的探索，导致设备节能和信号覆盖仍有改进空间。

Method: 采用随机几何理论构建分析模型，评估在蜂窝IoT网络中WUR-MIMO系统的唤醒成功概率，特别考虑天线数量设置为2×(设备数量)−1的配置。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟结果表明，相较于单天线WUR基准，MIMO波束成形大幅提高了唤醒可靠性，在所有设置下减少超过50%的错误激活，从而延长IoT设备使用寿命。

Conclusion: MIMO技术的应用有效解决了WUR在节能和覆盖方面的挑战，为未来IoT网络设计提供了理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: Wake-up radio (WUR) is a technology designed to enhance the energy efficiency of Internet of Things (IoT) networks and extend device battery life. While most studies focus on WUR performance with single-antenna base stations, this paper investigates the multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) technology to improve device energy saving and extend the coverage of wake-up signals. By leveraging MIMO beamforming, the transmitted energy can be spatially focused toward the intended IoT devices, with high beamforming gain and minimal inter-device interference. We develop a preliminary analytical framework using stochastic geometry to evaluate the wake-up success probability of WUR-MIMO in multi-cell cellular IoT networks, when the number of antennas equals $2 \times (\text{number of devices}) - 1$. Monte Carlo simulations show that, relative to a single-antenna WUR baseline, MIMO beamforming significantly enhances wake-up reliability when this antenna configuration is applied, mitigates more than 50% of false activations across all settings, and thereby prolongs the lifetime of IoT devices.

</details>


### [317] [Antenna System for Simultaneous Wireless Power and Information Transfer to Brain Implants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02036)
*Ali Khaleghi,Aminolah Hassanvand,Ilangko Balasingham*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无线脑机接口系统，通过双功能天线实现无电池供电与高速数据传输，解决传统BCI的有线连接及植入风险问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统植入式BCI依赖微电极阵列和有线连接，存在手术风险和维护复杂性，亟需开发更安全高效的无线解决方案。

Method: 设计双功能天线系统：1) 感应耦合机制实现无线电力传输，为ASIC芯片供能；2) 植入体内后向散射天线支持最高32Mbps无源高速数据传输。

Result: 实现无电池无线供电、32Mbps高带宽通信，在保证数据保真度的同时提升能源效率，适用于机械臂控制等高精度任务。

Conclusion: 该系统通过无线化设计显著提升BCI安全性与实用性，为神经康复和人机交互领域提供突破性技术路径。

Abstract: Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) have revolutionized neuroscience applications, from motor rehabilitation to neuroergonomics. Traditional implantable BCIs with invasive microelectrode arrays pose challenges, notably the need for wired connections and inherent implantation risks. This paper introduces a battery-free wireless BCI system, consolidating an implant and its external supporting system. Our design centers on a dual-function antenna system: firstly, an inductive coupling mechanism enables wireless power transfer, sufficiently powering the implant's Application-Specific Integrated Circuit (ASIC) for stimulation and readout without an implant battery. Secondly, a backscatter antenna in the implant facilitates battery-free, high-data-rate wireless connectivity (up to 32 Mbps). This system not only enhances the BCI experience by eliminating wires but also retains data fidelity and energy efficiency, promising a safer, more efficient interface for tasks like robotic arm control.

</details>


### [318] [Three-Dimensional Spatial Correlation Modeling for Cylindrical mMIMO Arrays in HAPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02111)
*Shasha Liu,Abla Kammoun,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Proposes a novel cylindrical-array SCF model using spherical harmonics and Fourier series to solve HAPS 3D channel modeling challenges, validated via simulations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing spatial correlation models fail to handle cylindrical antenna geometries essential for HAPS systems, creating a critical gap in 3D channel modeling for emerging ultra-wide coverage networks.

Method: The study derives an exact spatial correlation function (SCF) expression for cylindrical antenna arrays using spherical harmonic expansion (SHE) of plane waves, incorporating arbitrary radiation patterns and angular distributions via Fourier series (FS) coefficients of power azimuth/zenith spectra.

Result: The proposed SCF formulation achieves high accuracy in 3D MIMO channel modeling, validated through Monte Carlo simulations under standard-compliant configurations, with flexibility to accommodate diverse antenna characteristics and environmental conditions.

Conclusion: This work provides an exact closed-form SCF model for cylindrical MIMO arrays, enabling accurate 3D channel characterization for HAPS systems and addressing limitations of existing planar/linear array models.

Abstract: High-altitude platform stations (HAPS) are envisioned as a key component of future wireless networks, enabling ultra-wide coverage and providing direct connectivity to users with cylindrical massive multiple-input multiple-output (mMIMO) systems. Exploiting the channel degrees of freedom necessitates accurate modeling and characterization of three-dimensional (3D) channels in the presence of spatial correlation functions (SCFs). However, existing spatial correlation models are primarily developed for planar or linear antenna arrays and cannot be directly applied to cylindrical geometries commonly adopted by HAPS platforms. To address this limitation, this paper derives an exact closed-form expression for the SCF of 3D MIMO channels with antenna elements arranged in a cylindrical array. The proposed formulation is based on the spherical harmonic expansion (SHE) of plane waves and accommodates arbitrary antenna radiation patterns and angular distributions through the Fourier series (FS) coefficients of the power azimuth and zenith spectra. The derived SCF is validated through Monte Carlo simulations under standard-compliant settings.

</details>


### [319] [Coverage Analysis in Terahertz Clustered HetNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02125)
*Hadeel Obaid*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了太赫兹通信在聚类异构网络中的性能，通过随机几何方法证明采用泊松簇过程建模的用户/小基站分布相比泊松点过程具有更高覆盖率，且小基站的适度空间分散有利于提升覆盖效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太赫兹技术虽具备超大带宽潜力，但受限于短传输距离，需在异构网络中部署以增强覆盖范围；传统泊松点过程模型无法有效刻画用户聚集特性。

Method: 基于随机几何理论，构建双层异构网络模型：宏基站层采用泊松点过程(PPP)，小基站和用户层采用泊松簇过程(PCP)；推导用户关联概率、干扰拉普拉斯变换及覆盖概率解析表达式，并通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证。

Result: THz PCP-HetNets的覆盖率显著高于PPP-HetNets；数值结果表明小基站的适度空间扩散能优化覆盖性能。

Conclusion: 聚类异构网络架构结合太赫兹技术可实现更优覆盖性能，为下一代超宽带短距通信系统的设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Terahertz (THz) transmission technologies hold significant potential for enabling ultra-broadband, short-range communication in next-generation networks. Despite the vast bandwidth, THz signals suffer from limited transmission range and a feasible scenario is to deploy THz within clustered heterogeneous networks (HetNets) to enhance coverage. This paper investigates THz communication in clustered HetNets, leveraging stochastic geometry for performance analysis. Specifically, we consider two tiers of macro base stations (MBS) and small base stations (SBS). The MBS tier is modeled as a Poisson Point Process (PPP), and both the SBS tier and users are modeled as a Poisson Cluster Process (PCP) to capture user clustering and network hotspots. We derive the analytical expressions for user association probabilities, the Laplace transform of interference, and the coverage probability. The derived coverage probability is validated through Monte Carlo simulation. The numerical results show that the coverage in THz PCP-HetNets is higher than that achieved in THz PPP HetNets. In addition, a moderate spatial spread of SBSs is beneficial for coverage.

</details>


### [320] [Complexity-Scalable Direct Geolocation and Cancellation of Terrestrial GNSS Jammers: Single-Satellite and Multi-Antenna Experiments in Low Earth Orbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02190)
*Giacomo Pojani,Javier Tegedor,Joaquim Fortuny-Guasch,Francesco Menzione,David Evans,Tim Oerther,Maximilian Henkel,Jonas Stene Lindbjor*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究提出准直接定位（QDG）技术，通过压缩I/Q数据并结合快速时频域处理算法，实现对GNSS干扰源的高压缩比、近实时定位，有效解决低轨卫星资源受限问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卫星监测射频频谱需大量带宽和计算资源，但多数低轨卫星受尺寸、重量、功率（SWaP）限制。亟需一种高效技术复用现有卫星资源，实现GNSS频段干扰源的快速定位以支持多星座GNSS RFI监测系统。

Method: 提出QDG被动定位技术，包含：1) 多信号处理算法实现I/Q样本压缩；2) 量化时频域快速延迟-多普勒匹配与干涉测量；3) 单/多天线应用场景及多种压缩方法组合；4) 数学框架建模与精度边界分析。

Result: 基于OPS-SAT PRETTY卫星在Jammertest 2025采集的真实数据验证：在极高压缩比下仍可实现不同SNR的GNSS干扰源精准定位，突破下行链路和机载计算瓶颈。

Conclusion: QDG技术为低轨卫星网络提供了高效GNSS干扰监测解决方案，显著提升现有卫星资产利用率，对构建实时多星座RFI监测体系具有重要工程价值。

Abstract: Monitoring the radio-frequency (RF) spectrum from space imposes demanding requirements to satellite platforms in terms of communication bandwidth and computational resources, which are necessary for the downlink, the storage, and the processing of high-throughput I/Q samples. This paper analyzes in depth the quasi-direct geolocation (QDG) as a technique to enable the exploitation of satellites of opportunity in low Earth orbit (LEO) to sense the spectrum in the bands of global navigation satellite systems (GNSS). This is a technique of passive RF geolocation and consists of an ensemble of signal processing algorithms, which compress the I/Q samples and process the compressed data through fast delay-Doppler shift matching and interferometry in a quantized time-frequency domain. These algorithms speed up the exhaustive search of multiple RF sources in the position domain. The efficiency gain addresses the bottleneck that prevents the employment of satellites, which are limited in downlink capacity and on-board computational power. These satellites are usually constrained in size, weight and power (SWaP) and represent most of the spacecrafts in LEO. The ability to exploit assets as such for the geolocation of terrestrial GNSS jammers in near real time is instrumental the performance of a multi-constellation GNSS RFI monitoring system. The present work describes the mathematical framework and precision bounds, introduces single- and multi-antenna uses cases, combines different compression methods, and evaluates the geolocation accuracy with real data. The I/Q samples were collected by a repurposed GNSS reflectometry (GNSS-R) satellite, OPS-SAT PRETTY, in a dedicated test session during Jammertest 2025. The experimental results demonstrate the capability to geolocate GNSS jammers with different signal-to-noise ratios (SNR) with extremely high compression ratios.

</details>


### [321] [Fourier Preconditioning for Neural Feature Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02199)
*Preston Pitzer,Anish Pradhan,Harpreet S. Dhillon*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: FFT-based preconditioning significantly improves H-Score feature learning in data-scarce environments by concentrating predictive dependencies into fewer modes, validated through theoretical analysis and experiments demonstrating up to 50% error reduction with training-free performance prediction capabilities.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Addressing limitations of mutual information estimation in low-data scenarios and H-Score networks' vulnerability to input basis rotations under practical neural network architectures, which degrade feature extraction performance.

Method: Analysis of H-Score's rotational sensitivity under constrained approximation classes; development of unitary preconditioning strategies; identification of FFT as optimal data-independent preconditioner for stationary processes; design of spectral entropy and cumulative energy metrics for basis suitability assessment.

Result: Empirical validation across eight multivariate datasets shows 50% NMSE reduction via FFT preconditioning in resource-limited cases, with proposed metrics achieving strong correlation to actual performance gains and accurate prediction of ineffective preconditioning scenarios.

Conclusion: The study establishes that Fourier Transform-based preconditioning enhances H-Score networks' efficiency in low-resource regimes while introducing theoretically grounded, training-free metrics for predicting their efficacy without model training.

Abstract: Mutual information (MI)-inspired feature learning techniques are capable of generating low-dimensional embeddings that retain nonlinear dependence structures, but direct estimations of MI suffer from noisy probability distribution estimates in the low-data regime. The H-Score objective, computed from second-order statistics, provides a practical proxy metric for training feature extraction networks. We prove that H-Score is invariant to invertible transformations in the unrestricted functional setting, but becomes sensitive to input basis rotations under constrained approximation classes. Consequently, we study unitary preconditioning for H-Score networks and show that selecting an appropriate basis rotation reduces finite-width truncation error by concentrating predictive dependence into fewer dominant modes. We identify the fast Fourier transform (FFT) as an effective data-independent, low-cost preconditioner for approximately stationary processes, where spectral structure induces concentration of the cross-covariance singular value spectrum. We introduce training-free metrics based on spectral entropy and cumulative dependence energy to quantify basis suitability and predict downstream inference gains prior to network training. Experiments across eight multivariate datasets demonstrate that FFT preconditioning is particularly useful in resource-constrained regimes, achieving up to 50% normalized mean squared error (NMSE) reduction, while the proposed metrics correlate with observed performance gains and correctly identify cases where spectral preconditioning is detrimental.

</details>


### [322] [Beyond Beamforming: Phase-and-Gain Channel Shaping via Rotatable Antenna Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02305)
*Xingxiang Peng,Qingqing Wu,Ziyuan Zheng,Wen Chen,Guangchi Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可旋转天线阵列的几何重构传输方案，通过联合优化阵列姿态与单元波束指向，显著提升多用户通信系统的加权速率性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定天线阵列存在空间自由度受限问题，而旋转天线阵列可通过联合优化阵列姿态与单元级波束指向，同时优化信道相位响应与方向相关增益，从而突破现有系统性能瓶颈。

Method: 通过零强制分析揭示几何原理，并构建联合优化框架协调发射波束成形、阵列姿态调整与单元波束指向，以利用几何诱导的相位与增益信道塑造能力。

Result: 仿真实验表明，所提联合设计方案在加权速率指标上全面优于固定阵列、仅姿态优化及仅波束指向优化的基准方案，且在天线单元指向性增强或波束约束收紧时性能增益更为突出。

Conclusion: 本文提出的联合设计框架有效提升了多用户通信系统的性能，尤其在采用高指向性天线单元和严格波束约束条件下优势显著，为未来智能可重构天线阵列的设计提供了理论依据与实践指导。

Abstract: This paper investigates geometry-reconfigurable transmission for multiuser communication systems enabled by a rotatable antenna array. In contrast to conventional fixed arrays, the proposed architecture jointly exploits array pose adjustment and element-level boresight steering, thereby reshaping both the array-induced phase responses and the direction-dependent channel gains. We formulate a weighted sum-rate maximization problem that jointly optimizes the transmit beamformers, array pose, and element boresights under practical visibility and steering constraints. To reveal the underlying design principles, we first provide a geometric interpretation via zero-forcing analysis, showing that the resulting rates stem from both channel-strength enhancement and spatial-separability improvement. Specifically, array-pose rotation improves inter-user channel orthogonality even with isotropic elements, whereas directional elements introduce a tradeoff between phase-based spatial separation and boresight-dependent gain alignment. Motivated by these insights, we develop an efficient optimization framework that jointly coordinates transmit beamforming, array-pose adaptation, and element-boresight steering to exploit the geometry-induced phase-and-gain channel-shaping capability. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed joint design outperforms fixed-array, pose-only, and boresight-only benchmarks, with larger gains achieved under more directive element patterns and tighter boresight-steering constraints.

</details>


### [323] [Robust Transmission Design for RIS-Assisted RSMA-SWIPT Systems With Movable Antennas Under Hardware Distortions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02384)
*Muhammad Asif,Asim Ihsan,Irfan Muhammad,Mohd Hamza Naim Shaikh,Syed Tariq Shah,Zhu Shoujin,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对信道状态信息不确定性和硬件损伤环境下的多用户RSMA-SWIPT系统，提出结合可移动天线与智能反射面的鲁棒资源分配框架，通过联合优化波束成形、反射控制、功率分裂比和天线位置，显著提升系统可达和速率及鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RSMA-SWIPT系统在CSI不确定性和残留硬件损伤下存在信道耦合效应，导致波束成形、RIS反射控制、功率分配与天线位置优化高度耦合，传统方法难以有效应对由此产生的资源分配复杂性。

Method: 构建鲁棒优化框架，将原问题分解为活跃波束成形、RIS反射设计、功率分裂比优化和MA位置优化四个子问题，采用凸代理函数处理非凸约束，并设计交替迭代算法求解。

Result: 仿真表明所提方案在达到的和速率、对CSI不确定性和硬件损伤的鲁棒性以及收敛性能方面均优于基准方案，验证了联合优化的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究解决了复杂干扰环境下MA-RIS增强RSMA-SWIPT系统的鲁棒资源分配难题，为未来智能无线网络提供理论支撑和工程实现路径。

Abstract: This paper investigates a robust transmission design for a multi-user rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA)-based simultaneous wireless information and power transfer (SWIPT) system empowered by movable antennas (MAs) and a reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS) under channel state information (CSI) uncertainty and residual hardware impairments (HIs). The effective channels in MAs-enabled systems depend on antenna positions, causing CSI uncertainty to affect not only active and passive beamforming but also antenna position optimization. Furthermore, residual HIs distort the effective SINRs, creating additional coupling among beamforming, RIS reflection control, common-rate allocation, power-splitting ratio optimization, and antenna position optimization. Consequently, the joint impact of CSI uncertainty and HIs leads to a highly coupled and challenging resource allocation problem. To address this challenge, we propose a robust resource allocation framework that jointly optimizes common-rate allocation, transmit beamforming, RIS reflection coefficients, power-splitting ratios, and MAs positions to maximize the achievable sum-rate while satisfying practical system constraints. To obtain an efficient solution, the original problem is decomposed into active beamforming, RIS reflection design, power-splitting ratio optimization, and MAs position optimization subproblems, where tractable convex surrogate functions are constructed to handle the non-convex objective and constraints. Simulation results verify the effectiveness of the proposed framework and demonstrate substantial improvements in achievable sum-rate, robustness against CSI uncertainty and hardware impairments, and convergence performance compared with benchmark schemes.

</details>
